A) Alternating Minimization with dual weights and equality solving (KKT)
Given weights w_k (simplex), minimize f_w(a)=∑ w_k c_k over a≥0,sum=S. This is a quadratic with matrix Q_w. We can solve via projected gradient. But maybe we can solve exactly via eigen-decomposition? Q_w is a symmetric Toeplitz-like/Hankel matrix: (Q_w)_{ij} = 2w_{i+j} (if i≠j) plus w? Actually objective ∑ w_k c_k = ∑_{i,j} w_{i+j} a_i a_j. Matrix H with H_{ij}=w_{i+j}. Since w nonnegative, H is a Hankel matrix, symmetric positive? Is H_{ij}=w_{i+j} with w nonnegative. A Hankel matrix with nonnegative generating sequence is positive semidefinite? Let's check: x^T H x = ∑_{i,j} x_i x_j w_{i+j}. This equals ∑_k w_k (∑_{i+j=k} x_i x_j). The inner can be negative due cross terms, not necessarily nonnegative. Example n=2, w=(0,1,0), H=[[0,1],[1,0]] indefinite. So not PSD. But if w is completely monotone/Hankel PSD, then convex. Our w from softmax may not be PSD. But perhaps can restrict w to PSD Hankel to make convex. For KKT, w is simplex active but may not. Could use semidefinite parameterization w = h*h? Hmm.

If we choose w in such a way that H is PSD, the surrogate is convex. What w make H PSD? Sequence w_k nonnegative completely monotone? For all i,j, ∑_{i,j} w_{i+j} y_i y_j≥0 for all y. This is equivalent to a certain measure? If w_k are moments of a measure: w_k=∫ x^k dμ for k≥0 with x∈[?]? Then H_{ij}=∫ x^{i+j} dμ = (M M^T) PSD. But w length 2n-1 must be positive moments. Can we choose w as exp(-γ k)? Is that completely monotone? H = ∑_k e^{-γk} ∑_{i+j=k} y_i y_j. This is quadratic form with matrices e^{-γ(i+j)}? It equals ∫_0^∞ e^{-t} y(x?) Not PSD? e^{-γk} corresponds ∫_0^∞ e^{-(γ+k)t}? Then H_{ij}=∫ e^{-γt} e^{-i t} e^{-j t} dt, PSD! Yes for t positive, k=i+j -> e^{-γ(i+j)} = ∫_0^∞ e^{-t(γ+i+j)}? More precisely ∫_0^∞ e^{-t(γ+i+j)} dt =1/(γ+i+j). For e^{-γ(i+j)} discrete sum not integral exactly but can represent e^{-γ(i+j)} =? as Laplace of delta not positive? But sequence w_k=e^{-γ k} is positive definite? Toeplitz/Hankel? For Hankel matrix [1/(i+j+1)] is PSD (Hilbert). e^{-(i+j)}? Matrix [e^{-γ(i+j)}]=outer product (e^{-γi})(e^{-γj}) PSD. So if w_k = u_i u_j? w_k cannot be arbitrary product? For H_{ij}=w_{i+j}; to be PSD, need kernel k→w_k positive definite in additive semigroup. e^{γ k}? Since w_k = e^{?}; If w_k = e^{λ k}, matrix [e^{λ(i+j)}] = outer if λ real? e^{λ i} e^{λ j}, PSD (rank 1) for any real λ. But w must sum maybe. Infinite? For λ negative, sums small; positive matrix. Also w_k = ∫ e^{t(i+j)} dμ. So any positive definite sequence on N with Hankel kernel could. Example w_k = (1+n1)^{-k} maybe outer? Not exactly: (r)^{i+j}=r^i r^j yes if w_k = r^k with r<1. Can use convex surrogates with PSD w to ensure global minima. Maybe optimize f_r(a)=∑ r^k c_k over sum=S. Since H PSD, convex quadratic. Closed form? minimize a^T H a on simplex.   n=len(p)

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize as sp_min
# optional scipy.sparse, linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (max(1,m)-1).bit_length()
def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; L=nextpow2(size)
    A=fft(a,L); c=np.real(ifft(A*A)); return c[:size]
def compute_obj_from_c(a): ...
def compute_obj(a): ...
def softmax(h):
   h=np.asarray(h, float)
   m=np.max(h)
   e=np.exp(h-m)
   s=e.sum()
   return e/s
def conv_gradient_p(p,w):
   n=len(p); L=nextpow2(len(w)+n-1)
   pr=p[::-1]
   # fft w and pr
   c=np.real(ifft(fft(w,L)*fft(pr,L)))
   cross=c[n-1:n+n-1]
   return 2.0*cross
def logit_lse_value_grad(h,n,beta_c,symmetric=False):
    if symmetric: build full_h
    p=softmax(full_h)
    q=fft_convolve_self(p)
    qmax=np.max(q)
    beta = beta_c * 2.0*n
    # to avoid overflow: beta*qmax might large; subtract qmax before beta
    u = beta*(q - qmax)
    u -= u.max()
    e=np.exp(u)
    se=e.sum()
    # objective = 2n*(qmax + log(se)/beta)
    val = 2.0*n*(qmax + np.log(se)/beta)
    w=e/se
    gp=2.0*n*conv_gradient_p(p,w)
    mean = np.dot(p, gp)
    gfull = p*(gp - mean)
    if symmetric: combine
    return val,g
```
Need ensure beta not zero. For beta_c=0 exact max? LSE with beta inf; not for L-BFGS. Use beta_c at least 1.

But q - qmax maybe ~ -1e-6 to -1e-3; product -0.016 to -16. okay. If beta huge 1e7 and q differences 1e-5 product -100, fine. Underflow no issue. qmax ~1e-4; log(se)/beta ~ up to log(3n)/beta ~0.000003? val good.

```
def run_lp_direction_search(a_init, n, max_time, rng):
    S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize to S
    for iter...
       M=fft_convolve_self(a).max()
       if M<=0 break
       tol=1e-12*np.max(a)
       a_pos=a[a>tol]
       U=10.0*M/np.min(a_pos) if len else 1000
       # Build A for a (length n)
       A=build_conv_matrix(a,n)  # rows 2n-1, cols n
       res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=(0,U), method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':15?})
       highs may not have time_limit option? It has time_limit. But separate.
       if res.status==0:
           g=res.x; sumg=g.sum(); if sumg>0: g=1.0*g/S
           # line search t in [1,0.7,0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.01] from current a
           best_t=0; best_obj=compute_obj(a)
           for t in [1,0.8,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.05,0.01]:
              cand=(1-t)*a+t*g; obj=compute_obj(cand); update
           if best_t>0: a=best_cand
       if no improvement several times break
    return a
```

```
def resample_sequence(p_in, n_out):
    n_in=len(p_in); if n_in==n_out return p_in
    p_in=np.asarray(p_in,float); p_in=np.maximum(p_in,0); s=p_in.sum(); if s<=0 return None
    p_in=p_in/s
    if n_out>n_in:
       # Use cumulative trapezoid? To preserve integral, interpolate on [0,1] with endpoints at i/(n-1)? Step sequence as width 1. Use x_in=np.linspace(0,1,n_in,endpoint=False) maybe; np.interp with x_in, p_in, x_out, left=p_in[-1], right=p_in[0]. Normalizes.
    else:
       # downsample by averaging pooled? Use interpolation.
    return p_out normalized
```

```
def random_smooth_h(n, rng, sym=False):
   h=rng.normal(0,1,n)
   F=fft(h); cutoff=max(1,n//64); F[cutoff:-cutoff]*=0.01; h=real(ifft(F))
   h-=h.mean(); h*=0.5
```

```
def run_lbfgs_logit(...):
    m=...
    p = normalized p
    if symmetric: p = 0.5*(p+p[::-1])
    h0 = initial_half(p, B)
    best_h = h0.copy(); best_exact = exact_obj_for_h(h0,n,symmetric)
    total_budget = time_budget
    phase_deadline = time.monotonic()+total_budget
    # allocate beta by remaining proportion maybe equal
    for beta in beta_list:
       if time.monotonic() + 30 > phase_deadline: break
       # define globals
       _LB_N=n; _LB_BETA=beta; _LB_SYM=symmetric
       _CB_DEADLINE=phase_deadline-5
       _CB_MSG=f'lbfgs beta={beta}'
       maxiter = 30 if n<=10000 else 20
       res = sp_min(_logit_lbfgs_fun, h0.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=(B,B), callback=_callback_check, options={...})
       h1=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       exact=exact_obj_for_h(h1,...)
       if exact < best_exact: best_h=h1; best_exact=exact
       # perturb if no improvement? small
       if not improved:
           # maybe add smooth noise before next beta
           h1 = add_small_smooth_noise(h1, n, symmetric, rng?) # rng top-level? We can pass rng global
       h0 = h1
    return best_h, best_exact
```

Now exact_obj_for_h:
```
def exact_obj_h(h, n, symmetric):
  full = build_full_h(h,n,symmetric)
  p=softmax(full)
  q=fft_convolve_self(p)
  return 2.0*n*q.max()
```
This is called many times; for L-BFGS result evaluation only few. Good.

```
def build_conv_matrix_a(a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for k in range(L):
       j0=max(0,k-(n-1)); j1=min(n-1,k)
       length=j1-j0+1
       rows.append(np.full(length,k))
       cols.append(np.arange(j0,j1+1))
       data.append(a[k-j1:k-j0+1][::-1])
    return csr_matrix((np.concatenate(data), (np.concatenate(rows), np.concatenate(cols))), shape=(L,n))
```

`conv_gradient_p(p,w)`:
```
def conv_gradient_p(p,w):
    n=len(p)
    L=1 << (len(w)+n-2).bit_length() # len(w)+n-1
    # Actually if m=len(w)+n-1, L=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    yrev=p[::-1]
    c=np.real(ifft(fft(w, L) * fft(yrev, L)))
    return 2.0*c[n-1:n+n-1]
```
For n=1, length w=1, L=1, c length1, slice 0:1 ok.

Logit function:
Global: `_LB_N=0; _LB_BETA=1.0; _LB_SYM=False; _LB_SCALE=1.0`.
```
def logit_lse_value_full(h):
    n=_LB_N; beta_c=_LB_BETA; sym=_LB_SYM
    if sym: full=build_full_h(h,n,True)
    else: full=h
    p=softmax(full)
    q=fft_convolve_self(p)
    qmax=float(q.max())
    beta = beta_c * (2.0*n)
    # if beta < 1e-12? use max
    if beta < 1e-8:
       val = 2.0*n*qmax
       w=np.zeros(3*n-1; ) w[argmax]=1
    else:
       u=beta*(q-qmax)
       u-=u.max()
       e=np.exp(u)
       se=float(e.sum())
       val=2.0*n*(qmax + np.log(se)/beta)
       w=e/se
    gp=2.0*n*conv_gradient_p(p,w)
    mean=float(np.dot(p,gp))
    gfull=p*(gp-mean)
    if sym: grad=combine_grad(gfull,n)
    else: grad=gfull
    scale=_LB_SCALE
    return scale*val, scale*grad
```
Need if qmax maybe 0? If p positive, no.

`build_full_h(h,n,sym)`:
If not sym returns h. If sym:
```
m=(n+1)//2; full=np.empty(n); full[:m]=h
if n%2==0: full[m:]=h[:m][::-1]
else: full[m:]=h[:m-1][::-1]
return full
```
For n even, h length n/2. For odd, h length (n+1)//2.

`combine_grad(gfull,n)`:
```
m=(n+1)//2; if n%2==0: return gfull[:m]+gfull[m:][::-1]
else: gh=gfull[:m]+gfull[m:][::-1]; gh[m-1]=gfull[m-1]; return gh
```
Wait for odd, gfull[m:][::-1] length m-1? If gfull[m:] length n-m = m-1, reversed. gfull[:m] length m. Add align: gfull[0] with gfull[n-1], ..., gfull[m-2] with gfull[m]? Center gfull[m-1] not added. So:
```
gh = gfull[:m].copy()
if n%2==0: gh += gfull[m:][::-1]
else: gh[:m-1] += gfull[m:][::-1]
```
Good.

```
def run_one_lbfgs(h0, n, beta, symmetric, budget, B):
    _LB_N=n; _LB_BETA=float(beta); _LB_SYM=bool(symmetric); _LB_SCALE=float(n)
    _CB_DEADLINE=time.monotonic()+budget
    options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxls': 12, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-9, 'iprint':-1}
    try:
       res=sp_min(_logit_lbfgs_fun, h0.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=(B,B), callback=_lb_callback, options=options)
       return np.asarray(res.x,float)
    except Exception as e:
       print('L-BFGS error', e)
       return h0.copy()
```

Let's write FISTA. It will use `project_simplex` and `conv_gradient_p`. Use p sum1. Need objective exact. Use Newton-like topk.
```
def run_fista_p(p_init, time_budget, rng, beta_start=10.0, step_scale=1.0):
    n=len(p_init); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    p=normalize_p(p_init); y=p.copy(); x=p.copy(); t=1.0
    best_obj=compute_obj_p(y); best_p=y.copy(); stuck=0; it=0
    end=time.monotonic()+time_budget
    last_best=best_obj
    while time.monotonic()<end:
       it+=1
       q=fft_convolve_self(y); qmax=float(q.max()); obj=2.0*n*qmax
       if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
          best_obj=obj; best_p=y.copy(); stuck=0
       else: stuck+=1
       # beta
       beta = beta_start*(1.0 + 0.002*it)
       if beta > 5000: beta=5000
       # weights
       if stuck < 1000 or it % 50 ==0:
          beta_p=beta*2*n
          u=beta_p*(q-qmax); u=np.minimum(u,0); u=np.maximum(u,-700); e=np.exp(u); w=e/e.sum()
       else:
          k=min(32, max(2, stuck//200))
          idx=np.argsort(q)[-k:]; w=np.zeros_like(q); w[idx]=1.0/k
       grad=2.0*n*conv_gradient_p(y,w)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       step = step_scale/(beta*S)
       # sometimes random noise if stuck
       if stuck>2000 and (it%100==0):
          noise=rng.normal(0, 0.001*np.sqrt(1.0/n), n)
          # smooth? skip
          y=project_simplex(y+noise,1.0); t=1.0; x=y; stuck=0
       xn=project_simplex(y - step*grad,1.0)
       t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y_new = xn + ((t-1)/t_new)*(xn-x)
       y_new=np.maximum(y_new,0); s=y_new.sum(); if s<1e-12: y_new=xn
       else: y_new/=s
       x=xn; y=y_new; t=t_new
       if it % 5000 ==0: t=1.0
    return best_p,best_obj
```
Sort for topk each iteration expensive (16k log). But only when stuck>1000 and maybe rare. Use LSE weights mostly. LSE requires exp. Good.

This FISTA may be slow due project sort each iteration. For 120s maybe 1000 iterations due FFT 20ms +sort 1ms =? 2000. Fine.

```
def run_lp_small(a_init, n, time_budget, rng):
    if _linprog is None or _csr is None: return a_init
    S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize to S; best_obj=compute_obj(a); best_a=a; no=0
    end=now+time_budget
    while now<end-10:
       M=fft_convolve_self(a).max()
       if M <=0 or M<1e-12: break
       amin=a[a>1e-12*np.max(a)]
       U=10.0*M/amin.min() if len(amin)>0 else 100.0
       U=min(max(U,10.0),1e6)
       A=build_conv_matrix(a)
       res=_linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=(0,U), method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit': min(20.0, end-now-5)})
       if res is None or res.status !=0: break
       g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); s=g.sum(); if s<=1e-12: break
       g=g*(S/s)
       base_obj=best_obj
       best_t=0; best_cand=a.copy(); best_obj_local=base_obj
       for t in (1.0,0.8,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.05,0.01,0.003):
           cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
           cand=cand*cand.sum()?? Wait a and g sum S; blend sum S if t. No normalize needed but due numerical.
           cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000)
           obj=compute_obj(cand)
           if obj<best_obj_local: update
       if best_t>0:
          a=best_cand; best_obj=best_obj_local; best_a=a.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no+=1; if no>=3: break
       # update M
    return best_a
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  budget_s=max(0,float(budget_s))
  deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s-30
  best_obj=np.inf; best_p=None; best_n=0
  # get prev
  a_prev=None
  try:
     arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float).reshape(-1)
     arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000); if arr.sum()>0.01:a_prev=arr
  except: a_prev=None
  if a_prev is not None and len(a_prev)>0:
     a_prev_n=len(a_prev)
  else n_prev=8192
  n_core=8192
  if a_prev is not None:
     if 2048 <= len(a_prev) <= 16384: n_core=len(a_prev)
     else: n_core=8192
  # base p0 from prev or uniform
  if a_prev is not None:
     p_prev = normalize_p(resample_p(a_prev/n?.sum, n_core)) # resample density not sum S? resample expects density? Use p = a/sum.
  else: p_prev = uniform n_core
  candidates = generate_inits(n_core,rng,p_prev)
  # evaluate candidates
  for p in candidates:
      obj=compute_obj_p(p); if obj<best_obj update
      # sym version too
  # LP small maybe if a_prev or p_best
  p_for_lp = best_p if best_p is not None else p_prev
  if remaining>?? and _linprog:
     a_lp = np.sqrt(2*n_lp)*p_for_lp[:n_lp]? Need p length n_core; for LP n, downsample p_best to n.
     p_lp = resample_p(best_p, n_lp)
     a_lp = np.sqrt(2*n_lp)*p_lp
     a_res = run_lp_small(a_lp, n_lp, min(90, remaining*0.05), rng)
     p_lp_res=normalize_p(a_res)
     p_res=resample_p(p_lp_res,n_core)
     evaluate and sym, update best
  # FISTA on best p_core
  if remaining>180:
     budget_f= min(180, remaining*0.10)
     p_f,obj_f=run_fista_p(best_p,budget_f,rng)
     update
  # L-BFGS core
  if remaining>300:
     budget_core=remaining*0.45
     # full
     beta_schedule=[20,60,150,400,1000]
     h_res,obj_res=run_lbfgs_logit(best_p,budget_core*0.6,n_core,False,beta_schedule,10.0)
     update
     # sym
     remaining...
     h_sym,obj_sym=run_lbfgs_logit(sym(best_p),budget_sym,n_core,True,[30,100,300,800],10.0)
     update sym p
  # Secondary n2=16384 if remaining>300 and best_n<16384? Actually even if best_n=8192, upsample.
  n2=16384
  if remaining>300 and n_core<n2:
      p2=resample_p(best_p,n2)
      p2s=sym
      budget2=remaining*0.20
      run full/sym logit on p2 with beta [50,150,400]
      update if better
  # Final polish on best
  if remaining>60:
      # run lbfgs with high beta small time
      if len(best_p)==n2: n_final=len(best_p) else n_core
      budget_final=remaining
      beta_final=[200,500,1000]
      run full maybe
      if sym improves maybe
  # final list
  if best_p is None: uniform
  a_final = np.sqrt(2.0*len(best_p))*best_p
  a_final=np.clip(a_final,0,1000)
  # ensure sum > .01, if not scale
  s=a_final.sum(); if s<0.1: a_final*=0.1/s
  final=[float(x) for x in a_final]
  return final
```

Now, resample_p:
```
def resample_p(p_in, n_out):
   p_in=np.asarray(p_in,float).flatten(); p_in=np.maximum(p_in,0); s=p_in.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(n_out)/n_out
   p_in/=s
   n_in=len(p_in)
   if n_in==n_out: return p_in
   x_in=np.arange(n_in)/float(n_in)
   x_out=np.arange(n_out)/float(n_out)
   # To handle wrap, use extended arrays
   x_aug=np.concatenate((x_in, x_in+1.0))
   p_aug=np.concatenate((p_in, p_in))
   p_out=np.interp(x_out, x_aug, p_aug)
   p_out=np.maximum(p_out,1e-15)
   p_out/=p_out.sum()
   return p_out
```
If x_out includes 0, left from p_aug[0]=p0; if x_out approaches (n_out-1)/n <1, okay. No x=1. For x_out small, p_in[0]. Good. For high f, interpolation may create positive. Fine.

Implement `init_half_from_p` carefully:
```
def init_half_from_p(p, n, symmetric, B):
   p=normalize_p(p)
   if symmetric: p=sym_p(p)
   hfull=np.log(np.maximum(p*n,1e-12))
   hfull -= hfull.mean()
   if symmetric:
      m=(n+1)//2
      # average to enforce exact symmetric after log? p sym but numeric; average full with reversed
      hfull = 0.5*(hfull + hfull[::-1])
      hfull -= hfull.mean()
      return np.clip(hfull[:m], -B, B)
   else:
      return np.clip(hfull, -B, B)
```
If B too low and hfull outside, clipping may distort objective; okay.

Now, exact_obj_half:
```
def exact_obj_half(h,n,symmetric):
   full=build_full_h(h,n,symmetric)
   p=softmax(full)
   q=fft_convolve_self(p)
   return float(2.0*n*q.max())
```

`add_smooth_noise_half(h,n,symmetric,rng,scale)`:
- Generate full random if symmetric? We can generate half random smooth and mirror. Simpler: generate full noise length n, smooth, center, if symmetric mirror and take half. Use fft? For n=8192 inside few times okay.
```
def add_smooth_noise_half(h, n, symmetric, rng, scale):
    full_h=build_full_h(h,n,symmetric)
    noise=rng.normal(0,1,n)
    F=fft(noise)
    cut=max(2,n//50)
    F[cut:-cut]*=0.05
    noise=np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
    noise -= noise.mean()
    norm=np.linalg.norm(noise); if norm>0: noise=scale*noise/norm
    full_h = full_h + noise
    if symmetric:
       full_h=0.5*(full_h+full_h[::-1])
    h=full_h[: (n+1)//2] if symmetric else full_h
    return np.clip(h, -12,12)
```
Scale e.g., 0.05. This is a regular helper.

```
def _pow2_at_least(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

Now, `project_simplex`: Need if S<0? no. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    n=len(v)
    if S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(w)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta=(cum-S)/rho
    cond = theta <= w
    if not np.any(cond):
       idx=n
    else:
       idx=int(np.where(cond)[0].max())+1
    theta=(cum[idx-1]-S)/idx
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
If due roundoff sum slightly off, caller normalize.

Now, `normalize_p`:
```
def normalize_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).flatten()
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
       return np.ones(len(p))/len(p) if len(p)>0 else np.ones(1)
    return p/s
```
If len(p)=0? not.

Now, `compute_obj_p`:
```
def compute_obj_p(p):
   n=len(p); if n==0 return inf
   p=normalize_p(p) # or assume
   q=fft_convolve_self(p)
   return float(2.0*n*q.max())
```

`build_conv_matrix` using `csr_matrix`:
```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
    n=len(a)
    col_idx=np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
    offset=np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
    rows=col_idx+offset
    cols=col_idx
    data=np.repeat(a, n)
    return _csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
```
Check rows max: col_idx max n-1, offset max n-1 =>2n-2, okay. cols max n-1. data length n^2. For n=512, fine. This matrix rows order not sorted by row; csr handles. Some entries maybe duplicate? No, each pair (col,offset) unique. Good.

However, convolution A[k,j]=a[k-j] for 0≤k-j≤n-1. For column j and offset r=k-j, value a[r]. data repeat a for each column. Good.

LP constraints: `A_ub @ g` row k = ∑_j a[k-j] g_j = (a*g)_k. Good.

```
def _lb_callback(ck=None):
    if ck is not None:
```

```
def _add_candidate(lst, p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).flatten()
    if p.size==0: return
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
       return
    p = p/s
    # if any nan/inf, filter
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    s2=p.sum()
    if s2<=0: return
    lst.append(p/s2)
```

Implementation of `add_smooth_noise_half`:
```
def add_smooth_noise_half(h, n, symmetric, rng, scale):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    full=build_full_h(h,n,symmetric)
    noise=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    F=fft(noise)
    cut=max(2, n//50)
    if cut*2 < n:
        F[cut:-cut]*=0.05
    noise=np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
    noise-=noise.mean()
    nnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(noise))
    if nnorm > 1e-15:
        noise = scale * noise / nnorm
    full = full + noise
    if symmetric:
        full = 0.5*(full + full[::-1])
    if symmetric:
        m=(n+1)//2
        full_h=full[:m]
    else:
        full_h=full
    full_h -= full_h.mean()
    return np.clip(full_h, -12.0, 12.0)
```
If n small, cut maybe 2; F[2:-2] okay if n>4; if n smaller, skip. For n>=512.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
        if budget_s < 60: budget_s = 60.0
        deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, budget_s - 30.0)
        best_obj = np.inf
        best_p = None

        a_prev = None
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if np.isfinite(arr).all() and float(arr.sum()) > 0.01:
                a_prev = arr
        except Exception:
            a_prev = None

        if a_prev is not None and 2048 <= len(a_prev) <= 16384:
            n_core = int(len(a_prev))
        else:
            n_core = 8192
```

Let's check `run_fista_p` exactly:
```
def run_fista_p(p_init, time_budget, rng, beta_start=10.0, step_scale=1.0):
    p = normalize_p(p_init)
    n = len(p)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    x = p.copy(); y=p.copy(); t_mom=1.0
    best_obj=compute_obj_p(y); best_p=y.copy()
    stuck=0; it=0
    end=time.monotonic()+float(time_budget)
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it+=1
        q=fft_convolve_self(y); qmax=float(q.max()); obj=2.0*n*qmax
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj=obj; best_p=y.copy(); stuck=0
        else:
            stuck += 1
        beta = beta_start * (1.0 + 0.002*it)
        if beta < 2.0: beta=2.0
        if beta > 3000.0: beta=3000.0
        if stuck > 1500 and (it % 50 == 0):
            k = min(32, max(2, stuck//300))
            if k < q.size:
                idx = np.argpartition(q, -k)[-k:]
            else:
                idx = np.arange(q.size)
            w = np.zeros(q.size); w[idx] = 1.0/k
        else:
            beta_p = beta * (2.0*n)
            u = beta_p * (q - qmax)
            u = np.minimum(u, 0.0)
            u = np.maximum(u, -700.0)
            e = np.exp(u)
            se = float(e.sum())
            if se <= 1e-300:
                w = np.ones(q.size)/q.size
            else:
                w = e/se
        grad = 2.0*n*conv_gradient_p(y,w)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            break
        step = step_scale / (beta * S)
        if stuck > 2500 and (it % 200 == 0):
            noise_scale = 0.01 / np.sqrt(n)
            nz = rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
            y = project_simplex(y + nz, 1.0)
            y /= max(float(y.sum()), 1e-300)
            x = y.copy()
            t_mom = 1.0
            stuck = 0
            continue
        xn = project_simplex(y - step * grad, 1.0)
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0*t_mom*t_mom))
        y_new = xn + ((t_mom - 1.0) / t_new) * (xn - x)
        y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0)
        s = float(y_new.sum())
        if s < 1e-15:
            y_new = xn.copy()
        else:
            y_new = y_new / s
        x = xn
        y = y_new
        t_mom = t_new
        if it % 5000 == 0:
            t_mom = 1.0
    return best_p, best_obj
```
This uses `q.size` int. Good. We skip compute best every iteration? yes.

Potential: `np.argpartition(q, -k)` if k=0? k>=2. If q.size<k impossible n small? if n=2 q.size3, k maybe 2. Good.

```
def run_lp_small(a_init,n,time_budget,rng):
```

Add edge power: base=0.5+0.5cos2πt ≥0; raising r=3 small endpoints? okay.

Now, top candidates score: `compute_obj_p` normalizes copies. It will evaluate all patterns. Good.

```
if obj_sym < obj_final: best_p=sym_p(best_p); obj_final=obj_sym

```

```python
"""
Non-negative step-height search for minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a)/(sum(a)^2)

Method:
- Work with probability density p = a / sum(a).  Then the objective is
  2n*max(p*p).
- Use several carefully generated initial densities, including a resampled
  previous construction, symmetric/rolled variants, and smooth periodic
  candidates.
- Optional small linear-program direction step at small length, inspired by
  the previous SOTA but kept cheap and deterministic.
- Main polishing is done in logit variables h (p = softmax(h)) with an LSE
  approximation to max(p*p), optimized by bounded L-BFGS-B.  Both full and
  palindromic/symmetric subspaces are tried.
- Optional upscaling to length 16384 and final high-temperature polishing.

All helpers are top-level and do not use closures or lambda functions.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_min

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
except Exception:
    _linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix as _csr_matrix
except Exception:
    _csr_matrix = None


# Globals used by the scipy objective/callback wrappers.
_LB_N = 0
_LB_BETA = 1.0
_LB_SYM = False
_LB_SCALE = 1.0
_CB_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _pow2_at_least(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fft_convolve_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = _pow2_at_least(size)
    A = fft(a, L)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:size]


def normalize_p(p):
    """Project to the simplex p >= 0, sum(p) = 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def sym_p(p):
    """Symmetrize a density: p and reversed average."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    return normalize_p(0.5 * (p + p[::-1]))


def compute_obj(p):
    """Objective for a probability density p of length n."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    n = len(p)
    q = fft_convolve_self(p)
    return float(2.0 * n * q.max())


def compute_obj_from_a(a):
    """Objective for any non-negative a (same formula as evaluator)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    n = len(a)
    q = fft_convolve_self(a)
    mx = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def resample_p(p_in, n_out):
    """Resample a probability density to a new length using periodic linear interp."""
    p_in = normalize_p(p_in)
    n_in = len(p_in)
    if n_in == n_out:
        return p_in.copy()
    x_in = np.arange(n_in, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_in)
    x_aug = np.concatenate((x_in, x_in + 1.0))
    p_aug = np.concatenate((p_in, p_in))
    x_out = np.arange(n_out, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_out)
    p_out = np.interp(x_out, x_aug, p_aug)
    p_out = np.maximum(p_out, 1e-15)
    p_out = np.where(np.isfinite(p_out), p_out, 1e-15)
    s = float(p_out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        p_out = np.ones(n_out, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p_out = p_out / s
    return p_out


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(v)
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cumsum - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    theta = (cumsum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def softmax(h):
    """Numerically stable softmax."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    m = float(np.max(h))
    e = np.exp(h - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        n = len(h)
        if n == 0:
            return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def conv_gradient_p(p, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * (p*p)_k wrt p.
    Returns 2 * cross-correlation of w and p.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    L = _pow2_at_least(len(w) + n - 1)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    c = np.real(ifft(fft(w, L) * fft(p_rev, L)))
    cross = c[n - 1:n + n - 1]
    return 2.0 * cross


def _add_candidate(lst, p):
    """Append a finite nonnegative normalized candidate to lst."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size == 0:
        return
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return
    p = p / s
    if p.size > 0:
        lst.append(p)


def generate_initial_candidates(n, rng, p_prev):
    """Generate a small pool of diverse probability densities."""
    cands = []
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)

    if p_prev is not None:
        p_prev = normalize_p(p_prev)
        _add_candidate(cands, p_prev)
        _add_candidate(cands, sym_p(p_prev))
        # A few circular shifts / boundary alignments.
        for frac in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50):
            sh = int(round(frac * n))
            if sh != 0:
                _add_candidate(cands, np.roll(p_prev, sh))
                _add_candidate(cands, sym_p(np.roll(p_prev, sh)))

    # Smooth periodic U-shaped and higher-harmonic candidates.
    for a, b in (
        (0.30, 0.00), (0.50, 0.00), (0.70, 0.00), (0.90, 0.00),
        (0.30, 0.20), (0.55, 0.25), (0.80, 0.30), (1.00, 0.35)
    ):
        p = 1.0 + a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t) + b * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t)
        _add_candidate(cands, p)

    # Asymmetric low-center candidates.
    for a, b, c in (
        (0.80, 0.40, 0.20), (0.60, 0.30, -0.20), (1.00, 0.00, 0.00),
        (-0.50, 0.40, 0.10), (0.90, -0.30, 0.20), (0.30, -0.20, 0.10)
    ):
        p = (
            1.0
            + a * np.cos(np.pi * t)
            + b * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
            + c * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * t)
        )
        _add_candidate(cands, p)

    # Edge-heavy quadratics.
    for g in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        _add_candidate(cands, 1.0 + g * (2.0 * t - 1.0) ** 2)

    # Edge powers.
    base = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    for r in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        _add_candidate(cands, base ** r + 1e-10)

    # Smooth random log-normal shapes.
    for _ in range(4):
        h = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        F = fft(h)
        cut = max(2, n // 50)
        if cut < n // 2:
            F[cut:-cut] *= 0.05
        h = np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
        h -= h.mean()
        amp = 0.25 + 0.55 * rng.random()
        _add_candidate(cands, np.exp(h * amp))

    return cands


def build_full_h(h, n, symmetric):
    """Build full length-n logit vector from optional symmetric half-vector."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if not symmetric:
        return h
    m = (n + 1) // 2
    full = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    full[:m] = h
    if n % 2 == 0:
        full[m:] = h[:m][::-1]
    else:
        full[m:] = h[:m - 1][::-1]
    return full


def combine_gradient_half(gfull, n):
    """Map a full-length gradient to symmetric half-variable gradient."""
    m = (n + 1) // 2
    gh = gfull[:m].copy()
    if n % 2 == 0:
        gh += gfull[m:][::-1]
    else:
        gh[:m - 1] += gfull[m:][::-1]
    return gh


def p_from_h(h, n, symmetric):
    """Convert logit h to probability density p."""
    full = build_full_h(h, n, symmetric)
    return normalize_p(softmax(full))


def exact_obj_from_h(h, n, symmetric):
    """Exact objective 2n*max(p*p) from logit h."""
    return compute_obj(p_from_h(h, n, symmetric))


def logit_lse_value_full(h):
    """
    LSE smooth objective and gradient in logit variables.
    Uses global parameters _LB_N, _LB_BETA, _LB_SYM, _LB_SCALE.
    """
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(_LB_N)
    symmetric = bool(_LB_SYM)
    scale = float(_LB_SCALE)

    full_h = build_full_h(h, n, symmetric)
    p = softmax(full_h)
    q = fft_convolve_self(p)
    qmax = float(q.max())

    beta_c = float(max(1e-6, _LB_BETA))
    beta = beta_c * (2.0 * float(n))
    if beta < 1.0:
        beta = 1.0

    u = beta * (q - qmax)
    u = np.minimum(u, 0.0)
    u = np.maximum(u, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones_like(q, dtype=np.float64) / float(q.size)
        obj_base = 2.0 * float(n) * qmax
    else:
        w = e / se
        obj_base = 2.0 * float(n) * (qmax + np.log(se) / beta)

    gp = 2.0 * float(n) * conv_gradient_p(p, w)
    mean = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    gfull = p * (gp - mean)

    if symmetric:
        grad = combine_gradient_half(gfull, n)
    else:
        grad = gfull

    return float(scale * obj_base), scale * grad


def _logit_lbfgs_fun(h):
    return logit_lse_value_full(h)


def _lb_callback(callback_arg=None):
    if time.monotonic() > _CB_DEADLINE:
        return True
    return False


def init_half_from_p(p, n, symmetric, B):
    """Initial logit vector (symmetric half if requested)."""
    p = normalize_p(p)
    if symmetric:
        p = sym_p(p)
    hfull = np.log(np.maximum(p * float(n), 1e-12))
    hfull -= hfull.mean()
    if symmetric:
        hfull = 0.5 * (hfull + hfull[::-1])
        hfull -= hfull.mean()
        hfull = np.clip(hfull, -B, B)
        m = (n + 1) // 2
        return hfull[:m].copy()
    return np.clip(hfull, -B, B)


def add_smooth_noise_half(h, n, symmetric, rng, scale):
    """Add small smooth random noise to a logit vector."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    full = build_full_h(h, n, symmetric)

    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    F = fft(noise)
    cut = max(2, n // 50)
    if cut < n // 2:
        F[cut:-cut] *= 0.05
    noise = np.real(ifft(F))[:n]
    noise -= noise.mean()
    nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(noise))
    if nrm > 1e-15:
        noise = float(scale) * noise / nrm
    full = full + noise

    if symmetric:
        full = 0.5 * (full + full[::-1])

    if symmetric:
        m = (n + 1) // 2
        out = full[:m]
    else:
        out = full
    out -= out.mean()
    return np.clip(out, -12.0, 12.0)


def run_one_lbfgs(h0, n, beta, symmetric, budget, B):
    """Run one bounded L-BFGS-B subproblem in logit space."""
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    budget = float(max(3.0, budget))

    _LB_N = int(n)
    _LB_BETA = float(beta)
    _LB_SYM = bool(symmetric)
    _LB_SCALE = float(n)
    _CB_DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + budget

    bounds = [(float(B), float(B))] * len(h0)
    options = {
        "maxiter": 600,
        "maxls": 14,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "iprint": -1,
    }

    try:
        res = _sp_min(
            _logit_lbfgs_fun,
            h0.copy(),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=_lb_callback,
            options=options,
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.shape != h0.shape or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return h0.copy()
        return x
    except Exception as e:
        print("L-BFGS subproblem failed:", e)
        return h0.copy()


def run_lbfgs_logit(p_init, time_budget, n, symmetric, beta_schedule, B, rng):
    """
    Run a sequence of logit L-BFGS polishing stages.
    Returns best half/full logit vector and best exact objective.
    """
    p_init = normalize_p(p_init)
    n = int(n)
    nvars = (n + 1) // 2 if symmetric else n
    h0 = init_half_from_p(p_init, n, symmetric, B)
    if h0.size == 0:
        h0 = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_obj = exact_obj_from_h(best_h, n, symmetric)
    if not np.isfinite(best_obj):
        best_obj = np.inf

    betas = list(beta_schedule)
    if not betas:
        return best_h, best_obj

    total_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    end = time.monotonic() + total_budget
    elapsed = 0.0
    no_improve = 0

    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= end - 4.0:
            break
        remaining_total = max(0.0, end - now)
        left_phases = len(betas) - idx
        if left_phases <= 0:
            break
        frac = remaining_total / float(left_phases)
        phase_budget = max(5.0, frac - 1.0)
        if phase_budget > end - now - 2.0:
            phase_budget = max(3.0, end - now - 2.0)
        phase_start = time.monotonic()

        h1 = run_one_lbfgs(h0, n, beta, symmetric, phase_budget, B)
        obj1 = exact_obj_from_h(h1, n, symmetric)
        elapsed += time.monotonic() - phase_start

        improved = np.isfinite(obj1) and obj1 < best_obj - 1e-12
        if improved:
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_obj = obj1
            no_improve = 0
            print(
                "    logit L-BFGS sym=%s beta=%.0f obj=%.9f phase=%.1fs"
                % (symmetric, beta, obj1, elapsed)
            )
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            print(
                "    logit L-BFGS sym=%s beta=%.0f obj=%.9f (no improve)"
                % (symmetric, beta, obj1 if np.isfinite(obj1) else -1.0)
            )

        # For the next stage: usually keep the new point unless it got bad.
        if not np.isfinite(obj1) or obj1 > best_obj * 1.005 + 2e-5:
            h0 = best_h.copy()
        else:
            h0 = h1.copy()

        if no_improve >= 1:
            h0 = add_smooth_noise_half(h0, n, symmetric, rng, 0.06)

    return best_h, best_obj


def run_fista_p(p_init, time_budget, rng, beta_start=10.0, step_scale=1.0):
    """
    Auxiliary FISTA-like iteration directly in the simplex.
    Uses LSE smoothing and occasional exact top-k subgradients.
    """
    p = normalize_p(p_init)
    n = len(p)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    x = p.copy()
    y = p.copy()
    t_mom = 1.0
    best_obj = compute_obj(y)
    best_p = y.copy()
    stuck = 0
    it = 0
    end = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_budget))

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        q = fft_convolve_self(y)
        qmax = float(q.max())
        obj = 2.0 * float(n) * qmax
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_p = y.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        beta = beta_start * (1.0 + 0.002 * float(it))
        if beta < 2.0:
            beta = 2.0
        if beta > 3000.0:
            beta = 3000.0

        if stuck > 1500 and (it % 50 == 0):
            k = int(min(32, max(2, stuck // 300)))
            if k < q.size:
                idx = np.argpartition(q, -k)[-k:]
            else:
                idx = np.arange(q.size)
            w = np.zeros(q.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
        else:
            beta_p = beta * (2.0 * float(n))
            u = beta_p * (q - qmax)
            u = np.minimum(u, 0.0)
            u = np.maximum(u, -700.0)
            e = np.exp(u)
            se = float(e.sum())
            if se <= 1e-300:
                w = np.ones(q.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(q.size)
            else:
                w = e / se

        grad = 2.0 * float(n) * conv_gradient_p(y, w)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        step = float(step_scale) / (beta * S)

        # Small escape perturbation when stuck for a long time.
        if stuck > 2500 and (it % 200 == 0):
            noise_scale = 0.01 / np.sqrt(float(n))
            nz = rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
            y = project_simplex(y + nz, 1.0)
            s = float(y.sum())
            if s < 1e-15:
                y = x.copy()
            else:
                y = y / s
            x = y.copy()
            t_mom = 1.0
            stuck = 0
            continue

        xn = project_simplex(y - step * grad, 1.0)
        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t_mom * t_mom))
        y_new = xn + ((t_mom - 1.0) / t_new) * (xn - x)
        y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0)
        s = float(y_new.sum())
        if s < 1e-15:
            y_new = xn.copy()
        else:
            y_new = y_new / s
        x = xn
        y = y_new
        t_mom = t_new

        if it % 5000 == 0:
            t_mom = 1.0

    return best_p, best_obj


def build_conv_matrix(a):
    """
    Sparse matrix A such that A @ g == a * g (full linear convolution).
    Only used for small LP-inspired directions.
    """
    if _csr_matrix is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    col_idx = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
    offset = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
    rows = col_idx + offset
    cols = col_idx
    data = np.repeat(a, n)
    return _csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))


def run_lp_direction_small(a_init, n, time_budget, rng):
    """
    Cheap LP-inspired direction search at small length.
    Normalized to sum sqrt(2n), and returns the best a found.
    """
    if _linprog is None or _csr_matrix is None:
        return a_init.copy()

    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = len(a)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a * (S / s)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = compute_obj_from_a(best_a)
    no_improve = 0
    end = time.monotonic() + float(max(0.0, time_budget))

    while time.monotonic() < end - 8.0:
        c = fft_convolve_self(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-14:
            break

        amax = float(a.max())
        pos = a[a > 1e-13 * max(amax, 1e-300)]
        if pos.size == 0:
            U = 100.0
        else:
            U = float(min(max(10.0, 10.0 * M / float(pos.min())), 1e5))

        A = build_conv_matrix(a)
        if A is None:
            break

        try:
            res = _linprog(
                c=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=(0.0, U),
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print("LP direction solve failed:", e)
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-15:
            break
        g *= S / gs

        base_obj = best_obj
        best_t = -1.0
        best_cand = a.copy()
        best_obj_local = base_obj

        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.05, 0.02, 0.005):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cand * (S / cs)
            obj = compute_obj_from_a(cand)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj_local - 1e-13:
                best_obj_local = obj
                best_t = t
                best_cand = cand

        if best_t > 0.0:
            a = best_cand
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_obj = best_obj_local
            no_improve = 0
            print("  LP direction small n=%d improved to obj=%.8f" % (n, best_obj))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break

    return best_a


def consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p):
    """Update best objective/density, also considering symmetrization."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size == 0:
        return best_obj, best_p
    p = normalize_p(p)
    obj = compute_obj(p)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
        best_obj = obj
        best_p = p
    ps = sym_p(p)
    objs = compute_obj(ps)
    if np.isfinite(objs) and objs < best_obj:
        best_obj = objs
        best_p = ps
    return best_obj, best_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search function invoked by the grader.
    Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
        if budget_s < 60.0:
            budget_s = 60.0
        deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, budget_s - 30.0)

        best_obj = np.inf
        best_p = None

        # Load previous construction if available.
        a_prev = None
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(arr.sum()) > 0.01:
                a_prev = arr
        except Exception:
            a_prev = None

        # Primary search length. Prefer the provided length if reasonable.
        if a_prev is not None and 2048 <= len(a_prev) <= 16384:
            n_core = int(len(a_prev))
        else:
            n_core = 8192

        if a_prev is not None:
            p_prev = resample_p(a_prev / max(float(a_prev.sum()), 1e-15), n_core)
        else:
            p_prev = np.ones(n_core, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_core)

        # Initial candidates and their quick exact objective.
        cands = generate_initial_candidates(n_core, rng, p_prev)
        scored = []
        for i, p in enumerate(cands):
            obj = compute_obj(p)
            if np.isfinite(obj):
                # Tuple sorting avoids lambda; unique index prevents array comparison.
                scored.append((obj, i, p))
        if scored:
            scored.sort()
            top_p = [p for (_, _, p) in scored[:8]]
            if top_p:
                best_p = top_p[0]
                best_obj = scored[0][0]
        else:
            top_p = [p_prev]
            best_p = normalize_p(p_prev)
            best_obj = compute_obj(best_p)

        best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, best_p)
        print("Initial best: n=%d, obj=%.9f" % (len(best_p), best_obj))

        # Optional cheap LP-inspired direction at small length.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 700.0 and _linprog is not None and _csr_matrix is not None:
            lp_budget = min(100.0, rem * 0.045)
            if lp_budget > 25.0:
                n_lp = 1024 if rem > 1200 else 512
                p_lp = resample_p(best_p, n_lp)
                a_lp = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_lp)) * p_lp
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                a_lp_out = run_lp_direction_small(a_lp, n_lp, lp_budget, rng)
                p_lp_out = normalize_p(resample_p(a_lp_out, n_core))
                best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_lp_out)
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                print("After LP small direction: obj=%.9f, t=%.1fs" %
                      (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0 + time.monotonic() - time.monotonic()))

        # One auxiliary FISTA-style simplex refinement.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 400.0:
            f_budget = min(120.0, rem * 0.05)
            if f_budget > 20.0:
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                p_f, obj_f = run_fista_p(best_p, f_budget, rng, beta_start=10.0, step_scale=1.0)
                best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_f)
                print("After FISTA simplex: obj=%.9f, t=%.1fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

        # Main core polishing by logit L-BFGS at the primary length.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 250.0 and best_p is not None and len(best_p) == n_core:
            core_budget = rem * 0.48
            print("\nCore logit L-BFGS budget: %.1fs" % core_budget)

            # Primary full-space polish.
            budget1 = core_budget * 0.55
            h_res, obj_res = run_lbfgs_logit(
                best_p, budget1, n_core, False,
                beta_schedule=(20.0, 60.0, 150.0, 400.0, 1000.0),
                B=10.0, rng=rng
            )
            p_res = p_from_h(h_res, n_core, False)
            best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_res)
            print("After core full L-BFGS: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

            # Symmetric-space polish.
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            budget2 = max(0.0, min(core_budget * 0.30, rem * 0.4))
            if budget2 > 20.0:
                h_sym, obj_sym = run_lbfgs_logit(
                    sym_p(best_p), budget2, n_core, True,
                    beta_schedule=(30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 800.0),
                    B=10.0, rng=rng
                )
                p_sym = p_from_h(h_sym, n_core, True)
                best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_sym)
                print("After core symmetric L-BFGS: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

            # A second distinct initial candidate if it was near best initially.
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if len(top_p) > 1 and rem > 120.0:
                p_second = normalize_p(top_p[1])
                obj_second = compute_obj(p_second)
                if np.isfinite(obj_second) and obj_second < best_obj * 1.015 + 2e-3:
                    budget3 = max(0.0, min(core_budget * 0.18, rem * 0.3))
                    if budget3 > 20.0:
                        h2, obj2 = run_lbfgs_logit(
                            p_second, budget3, n_core, False,
                            beta_schedule=(30.0, 100.0, 300.0),
                            B=10.0, rng=rng
                        )
                        p2 = p_from_h(h2, n_core, False)
                        best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p2)
                        print("After second-init L-BFGS: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

        # Optional upscaling to 16384.
        n_up = 16384
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_p is not None and len(best_p) < n_up and rem > 450.0:
            up_budget = rem * 0.22
            print("\nUpscale to n=%d budget: %.1fs" % (n_up, up_budget))
            p_up = resample_p(best_p, n_up)

            h_up, obj_up = run_lbfgs_logit(
                p_up, up_budget * 0.60, n_up, False,
                beta_schedule=(50.0, 150.0, 400.0),
                B=10.0, rng=rng
            )
            p_up_out = p_from_h(h_up, n_up, False)
            best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_up_out)
            print("After upscale full L-BFGS: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 80.0:
                up_budget2 = max(0.0, min(up_budget * 0.40, rem * 0.4))
                if up_budget2 > 15.0:
                    h_ups, obj_ups = run_lbfgs_logit(
                        sym_p(p_up), up_budget2, n_up, True,
                        beta_schedule=(80.0, 250.0, 700.0),
                        B=10.0, rng=rng
                    )
                    p_ups = p_from_h(h_ups, n_up, True)
                    best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_ups)
                    print("After upscale symmetric L-BFGS: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

        # Final high-sensitivity polish.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_p is not None and rem > 60.0:
            n_final = len(best_p)
            if n_final <= 16384:
                final_budget = rem * 0.65
                h_fin, obj_fin = run_lbfgs_logit(
                    best_p, final_budget, n_final, False,
                    beta_schedule=(200.0, 500.0, 1000.0),
                    B=10.0, rng=rng
                )
                p_fin = p_from_h(h_fin, n_final, False)
                best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_fin)
                print("Final full polish: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 40.0:
                    h_fins, obj_fins = run_lbfgs_logit(
                        sym_p(best_p), rem * 0.7, n_final, True,
                        beta_schedule=(300.0, 800.0, 1500.0),
                        B=10.0, rng=rng
                    )
                    p_fins = p_from_h(h_fins, n_final, True)
                    best_obj, best_p = consider_candidate(best_obj, best_p, p_fins)
                    print("Final symmetric polish: obj=%.9f" % best_obj)

        # Final sanity and conversion to returned list.
        if best_p is None:
            n_fb = 8192
            best_p = np.ones(n_fb, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_fb)
            best_obj = compute_obj(best_p)

        # One last cheap sym check.
        ps = sym_p(best_p)
        objs = compute_obj(ps)
        if objs < compute_obj(best_p):
            best_p = ps
        best_p = normalize_p(best_p)
        S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(len(best_p)))
        a_final = S * best_p
        a_final = np.where(np.isfinite(a_final), a_final, 0.0)
        a_final = np.clip(a_final, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s_final = float(a_final.sum())
        if s_final < 0.1:
            a_final = np.ones(len(a_final), dtype=np.float64)
            a_final = a_final * (0.1 / float(a_final.size))
        final_obj = compute_obj_from_a(a_final)

        print("\nReturning sequence: n=%d, final objective by FFT=%.9f" %
              (len(a_final), final_obj))

        return [float(x) for x in a_final]

    except Exception:
        n_fb = 8192
        a_fb = np.ones(n_fb, dtype=np.float64) * (np.sqrt(2.0 * n_fb) / float(n_fb))
        return [float(x) for x in a_fb]
```
